			<title><![CDATA[Rubrik CTO: The End of Zero Trust?]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p><a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#">Arvind Nithrakashyap</a> is the Co-Founder and CTO of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#">Rubrik</a> (NYSE: RBRK) the cybersecurity resilience platform.</p><p><br></p><p>We started by exploring how <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#">Rubrik</a> has evolved from a data backup and protection company into a <strong>#CyberResilience</strong> and data security platform - and what&#39;s next in the new AI era and RAG technology.</p><p><br></p><p>And indeed the world is changing, Enterprise security was built around deterministic policies: do X if Y happens, while <strong>#LLMs</strong> are probabilistic - So what happens to the <strong>#ZeroTrust</strong> principles?</p><p><br></p><p>We also discussed <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#">Rubrik</a> <strong>#AgentRewind</strong>, which tackles mistakes made by autonomous AI <strong>#agents</strong>. We dove into how <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#">Arvind</a>’s team built the audit trail needed to determine whether an incident was caused by a flawed AI prompt, an autonomous agent’s decision, or a compromised user credential.</p><p><br></p><p>Other interesting topics we covered:</p><p>- How <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#">Arvind</a> measures <strong>#ROI</strong> in the new AI era</p><p>- Cybersecurity &amp; Open Source</p><p>- Cybersecurity &amp; Quantum computing</p><p>- Tokenmaaxing</p><p><br></p><p>And <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#">Arvind</a>’s favorite movie? 🤌💼👨‍👩‍👧‍👦</p><p><br></p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br></p><p>Register for our newsletter</p><p>https://boardroomclub.substack.com</p><p><br></p><p>Host: <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#">Yossi Garmazi</a></p><p><br></p><p><strong>#ai</strong> <strong>#tech</strong> <strong>#cybersecurity</strong> <strong>#coding</strong> <strong>#agent</strong></p><p><br></p><p><strong>Chapters:</strong></p><p>00:00 Cybersecurity &amp; AI Agents</p><p>00:32 Intro</p><p>01:04 IIT Lessons</p><p>03:33 India Rise as a Tech Hub</p><p>04:48 Open Source &amp; Cybersecurity </p><p>08:34 What&#39;s next for Rubrik? 			<title><![CDATA[Chainguard CEO: Can AI Break Open Source?]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Dan Lorenc is the Co-Founder of Chainguard, which raised ~$900M to make it Open Source secure to use.</p><p><br></p><p>We started by discussing the <strong>#challenges</strong> around the distribution and consumption of open source. It’s free and ubiquitous - but do we really consume the source code itself?</p><p><br></p><p>Or as Dan phrase it:</p><p>"It's free as in puppy, not free as in beer"</p><p><br></p><p>We then explored <strong>#ChainguardImages</strong>, what the future holds for <strong>#containers</strong> in the new era of AI, and whether the industry is heading toward a more standardized, centralized system.</p><p><br></p><p>Other interesting topics we covered:</p><p>- Was <strong>#Mythos</strong> a marketing stunt?</p><p>- Chainguard Agent Skills and agent-to-agent attacks</p><p>- The beginning of Chainguard</p><p><br></p><p>And who would <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Dan</a> would love to grab a coffee with? 🏀🗽👑</p><p><br></p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br></p><p>Register for our newsletter</p><p>https://boardroomclub.substack.com</p><p><br></p><p>Host: Yossi Garmazi</p><p><br></p><p>#ai #tech #cybersecurity #coding #agent #knicks #JalenBrunson</p><p><br></p><p><strong>Chapters:</strong></p><p>00:00 Open Source & AI</p><p>00:27 Intro</p><p>00:58 What is Open Source?</p><p>02:46 Agents & Open Source</p><p>05:28 Is Open Source broken?</p><p>08:08 The Beginning of Chainguard</p><p>09:07 Chainguard Base-image & Containers</p><p>10:29 Open Source Regularity Pressure</p><p>11:34 Mythos: A Marketing Stunt?</p><p>12:46 Agent-to-Agent Attack</p><p>14:41 Open Source Misconception</p><p>16:10 Execution in Startups</p><p>17:00 When to Hire Sales?</p><p>18:28 Advice for Builders</p><p>19:44 Tokenmaaxing</p><p>20:56 What is AI Native Company?</p><p>21:26 Mentorship</p><p>22:08 Coffee Question</p><p>23:00 Favorite Movie</p><p><br></p>

			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Itamar Sivan, Ph.D.</a>, Co-Founder and CEO of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Quantum Machines</a>.</p><p><br></p><p>The quantum space is having some serious momentum, and one of the companies at the center of it is <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Quantum Machines</a>.</p><p><br></p><p>We started by discussing what <strong>#quantum</strong> computing is all about, and how <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Quantum Machines</a> is changing the game with its <strong>#orchestration</strong> layer for quantum processors.</p><p><br></p><p>One of their latest product launches, the <strong>#OPX1000</strong>, signals a shift toward the kilo-qubit era. As such, we explored some of the key challenges involved, particularly the need to perform large-scale computations at low latency.</p><p><br></p><p>And this is <strong>#complex</strong>, In fact, quantum processors with 300 qubits, from a potential data storage perspective, are like a classical processor with more bits than the atoms (!) in the universe.</p><p><br></p><p>And people are paying attention, as we also touched on their <strong>#DGX</strong> Quantum collaboration with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">NVIDIA</a> and what it signals about the future direction of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Quantum Machines</a> and the broader ecosystem.</p><p><br></p><p>Since quantum technology is still evolving, we also discussed the <strong>#business</strong> side of the field:</p><p>- How to communicate ROI to investors</p><p>- How to evaluate progress in milestones rather than immediate revenue</p><p><br></p><p>Lastly, we asked for <strong>#advice</strong> for entrepreneurs building in deep tech.</p><p><br></p><p>And <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/?_bprMode=vanilla#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Itamar</a>’s favorite <strong>#movie</strong> franchise? 			<title><![CDATA[Glean CEO: Save 50% of Tokens Using Glean | Apex Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Arvind Jain</a>, Founder and CEO at <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Glean</a>.</p><p><br></p><p><a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Glean</a> has raised over $770M and is valued at $7.2B, with a mission to unlock enterprise knowledge.</p><p><br></p><p>As they cracked the enterprise <strong>#search</strong> space, we started by discussing <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Glean</a>&#39;s early <strong>#contrarian</strong> bets - from defining the problem differently to building a single-tenant architecture that could be easily deployed within a customer&#39;s environment.</p><p><br></p><p>And <strong>#growth</strong> is booming. We explored the key differentiators that helped Glean scale from roughly $100M ARR at the beginning of 2025 to more than $300M today.</p><p><br></p><p>But besides being a search tool, we discussed how <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Glean</a> is also reshaping the AI <strong>#token</strong> economy by providing critical context to AI models, helping customers reduce token consumption by as much as 50% in some cases.</p><p><br></p><p>We then dove into <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Glean</a>&#39;s <strong>#AgenticEngine2</strong>, and touched on why product they give users direct control over AI reasoning power.</p><p><br></p><p>Other topics we covered:</p><p>• How AI has changed hiring at Glean</p><p>• What launching <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Google</a> <strong>#MapMaker</strong> in <strong>#India</strong> taught Arvind about building and scaling data platforms</p><p><br></p><p>And <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Arvind</a>&#39;s favorite <strong>#movie</strong>? Users - especially <strong>#GenZ</strong> - increasingly prefer visual discovery over text-based search, positioning Pinterest naturally at the center of this transition.</p><p><br></p><p>While <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Pinterest</a> has long been known for visual search, the company is now expanding into <strong>#ConnectedTV</strong> following the acquisition of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">tvScientific</a>. 			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Steve Leightell</a>, Lead Investor of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Georgian</a>.</p><p><br></p><p><a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Georgian</a> has invested in some of the most well-known cybersecurity companies out there -  including <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Armis</a>, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Island</a>, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Cyera</a>, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Chainguard</a>, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Sublime Security</a> and others.</p><p><br></p><p>We started  by exploring one of their biggest differentiators: its <strong>#AILab</strong>, comprised of more than 30 professionals ranging from PhDs and researchers to full-stack engineers, working directly with portfolio companies to tackle the latest challenges - and opportunities - emerging across <strong>#cybersecurity</strong> and AI.</p><p><br></p><p>From there, we discussed the new security risks introduced by AI tools like <strong>#OpenClaw</strong>, and the opportunity for a new generation of cyber companies to emerge - potentially becoming the ultimate <strong>#ZeroTrust</strong> gatekeepers of the AI era.</p><p><br></p><p>Today, more than ever, we’re seeing <strong>#platformization</strong> across the market, with large players acquiring capabilities to build end-to-end security platforms. But does that mean there’s no longer room for highly focused companies solving one specific problem exceptionally well?</p><p><br></p><p>We used <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Sublime Security</a> as a case study, diving into its open architecture approach and how it is rethinking email security for the modern threat landscape.</p><p><br></p><p>Finally, we explored <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Georgian</a>’s “Crawl, Walk, Run” framework - and how they help portfolio companies move from experimentation to scale, when AI begins directly driving net retention and ARR growth.</p><p><br></p><p>And guess what’s <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Steve</a>’s alternate-universe <strong>#profession</strong>? 🌱🧑‍🌾🚜</p><p><br></p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br></p><p>Register for our newsletter</p><p>https://boardroomclub.substack.com</p><p><br></p><p>Interviewers: <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Yossi Garmazi</a> &amp; So we decided to explore how it <strong>#scaled</strong> so quickly.</p><p><br></p><p>We started by discussing what sets <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Vanta</a> apart in such a competitive market. As <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Jeremy</a> explained, it comes down to <strong>#automation</strong>, deep integrations, a unified view of risk, and strong first-mover advantage.</p><p><br></p><p>A few staggering numbers: Vanta has 400 <strong>#integrations</strong>, 1,300 automated tests, and ships around 25 new <strong>#features</strong> every month!</p><p><br></p><p>To move at that pace, the company advocates for fewer decisions by <strong>#committee</strong> and more decisive action. 			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Chris Koehler</a>, Chief Marketing Officer at <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Twilio</a> (NYSE: TWLO).</p><p><br></p><p>When you think about <strong>#marketing</strong>, you think about <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Twilio</a>. But what does marketing actually look like in the AI era?</p><p><br></p><p>We started by exploring the space and how <strong>#marketing</strong> has evolved in the last five years. Interestingly, in a world where AI makes it easier than ever to execute campaigns, <strong>#brand</strong> has become even more important.</p><p><br></p><p>This led us to discuss their in-house <strong>#AI</strong> agent, <strong>#Isa</strong>, which now handles over 95% of inbound requests. It’s a game changer, enabling near-unlimited scale while delivering a completely different kind of <strong>#customer</strong> experience.</p><p><br></p><p>Another great product we touched on is <strong>#TwilioSegment</strong> , and how it helps bridge the gap between a brand’s <strong>#perception</strong> and the actual customer reality.</p><p><br></p><p>Lastly, we asked a bigger question around the future of <strong>#marketing</strong>: who are we really marketing to - humans or <strong>#agents</strong>? 			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#">Niko Bonatsos</a>, Founder & MD at <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#">Verdict Capital</a>, Fmr. 			<title><![CDATA[Snowflake CRO + CMO: The $3B GTM Machine | Builders Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Chris Degnan</a>, Founding CRO, and <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Denise Persson</a> CMO of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Snowflake</a> (NYSE: SNOW).</p><p><br></p><p>Everybody knows <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Snowflake</a>❄️ - a <strong>#Cloud</strong> and AI powerhouse generating over $3B in revenue. But what actually drives this <strong>#GTM</strong> machine?</p><p><br></p><p>We started by discussing who <strong>#leads</strong> the way - the revenue team or the marketing team?</p><p><br></p><p>It turns out, it’s all about <strong>#collaboration</strong>. From the early days of sending 5,000 <strong>#ColdEmails</strong> a week, to evolving into account-based <strong>#marketing</strong> with highly customized messaging for each customer, to building a strong reference system.</p><p><br></p><p>We then explored whether <strong>#grit</strong> still <strong>#matters</strong> in a world where AI automates everything. Ironically, when every <strong>#AI</strong>-generated message sound the same, grit becomes a true differentiator. Sometimes, it’s as simple as picking up the phone and calling customers.</p><p><br></p><p>🤖 New Phase: ✨</p><p><br></p><p>As <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Snowflake</a> is known as a leading cloud company - so how did it successfully position itself as an <strong>#AI</strong> player? And more importantly, how does it <strong>#StandOut</strong> when every company claims to be “AI-first”?</p><p><br></p><p>It&#39;s all about working closely with <strong>#customers</strong>. It seems to work out, with major <strong>#partnerships</strong> - including a recent $400M collaboration with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">OpenAI</a> and <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Anthropic</a> - and the launch of cutting-edge initiatives like <strong>#ProjectSnowWork</strong>.</p><p><br></p><p>Lastly, We also discussed how their <strong>#ICP</strong> is evolving. In this new era, <strong>#BusinessUsers</strong> and end users are increasingly becoming new key decision-makers.</p><p><br></p><p>And guess what <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Chris</a> and <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Denise</a>’s favorite <strong>#book</strong> is? ❄️📚☃️</p><p><br></p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br></p><p>Check out our new YouTube Channel:</p><p>https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub</p><p><br></p><p>Interviewers: <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Yossi Garmazi</a> &amp; <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#">Álvaro Jiménez Cabello de los Cobos</a>.</p><p><br></p><p><strong>#AI</strong> <strong>#Cloud</strong> <strong>#GTM</strong> <strong>#marketing</strong> <strong>#product</strong></p><p><strong>Chapters:</strong></p><p>00:00 Intro</p><p>01:00 Joining Snowflake</p><p>03:53 Aligning Sales &amp; Marketing</p><p>05:51 Early days in sales</p><p>08:27 Approach to Marketing</p><p>12:47 Marketing as part of a sales engine</p><p>16:39 Grit in the automation era</p><p>19:21 Branding Snowflake</p><p>24:50 Selling AI products</p><p>28:06 Snowflakes SAAS model</p><p>29:46 Rewriting ICP in the AI era</p><p>34:06 re-Building on the Snowflake platform</p><p>38:35 Lessons from and updates to &quot;Make it Snow&quot;</p><p>40:24 Advice for entrepreneurs</p><p>44:15 Favorite Book</p><p>46:10 Alternative Career</p>

			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Philip Guido</a>, EVP and Chief Commercial Officer at <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">AMD</a> (NASDAQ: AMD).</p><p><br /></p><p>Just in the past couple of weeks, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">AMD</a> set the tone with $100B+ <strong>#deals</strong> being signed. So this time, we decided to explore the <strong>#GTM</strong> engine behind it all.</p><p><br /></p><p>We started by discussing how relationships with <strong>#hyperscalers</strong> have evolved - from traditional vendor dynamics to true co-creation <strong>#partnerships</strong>, like in the <strong>#EPYC</strong> case, while keep full transparency throughout the process.</p><p><br /></p><p>From there, we explored how <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">AMD</a>’s unified <strong>#sales</strong> structure of Data Center, Embedded, and Client sales — enables stronger cross-selling into <strong>#Fortune500</strong> companies, especially as AI workloads increasingly move from the cloud to the edge.</p><p><br /></p><p>A major <strong>#differentiator</strong> for AMD is its <strong>#OpenEcosystem</strong> approach. We touched into why open source and optionality matter so much, and at the end drive innovation, competition, choice, and pricing flexibility.</p><p><br /></p><p>With <strong>#AI</strong> now moving from the enterprise to the <strong>#edge</strong>, we used <strong>#RyzenPRO</strong> as a case study to understand how the GTM team gets involved early in the development cycle— as time to market could take years in the space.</p><p><br /></p><p>We also covered the key <strong>#metrics</strong> <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Philip</a> constantly tracks—pipeline growth, POC, and win rates. 			<title><![CDATA[Capillary Technologies CEO: Loyalty Programs & AI | Builders Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Aneesh Reddy</a>, Founder and CEO of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Capillary Technologies</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p>As the <strong>#AI</strong> revolution accelerates, customers increasingly expect <strong>#personalized</strong> experiences. One company right at the center of it is <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Capillary Technologies</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p>We began with Capillary’s early days, just before the 2008 financial crisis. <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Aneesh</a> shared how they <strong>#navigated</strong> the tough times by focusing on <strong>#loyalty</strong> programs and helping SMBs in India - later <strong>#expanding</strong> globally to boost customer retention and growth.</p><p><br /></p><p>From there, we explored how Capillary evolved from working primarily with <strong>#retailers</strong> to serving multiple verticals and large <strong>#enterprises</strong>, as well as the strategic decision to expand into the <strong>#US</strong> and EMEA markets.</p><p><br /></p><p>We also discussed the broader <strong>#India’s</strong> technology <strong>#ecosystem</strong> - from an outsourcing-led industry to a global innovation <strong>#powerhouse</strong>, with strong R&amp;D centers, application infrastructure, and increasingly, deep-tech leadership.</p><p><br /></p><p>A major milestone for the company has been its recent <strong>#IPO </strong>(November 2025), which was <strong>#oversubscribed</strong> more than 50X. We touched on why they chose to go public in <strong>#India</strong> and the key factors behind the successful offering.</p><p><br /></p><p>We also touched on <strong>#competition</strong>, Capillary’s <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Adobe</a> partnership, and how they stand out with AI loyalty agents and rapid <strong>#SpeedToMarket</strong>.</p><p><br /></p><p>Finally, we discussed the evolving <strong>#loyalty</strong> landscape, the <strong>#gamification</strong> of it, and <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Capillary Technologies</a>’s future <strong>#expansion</strong> plans.</p><p><br /></p><p>And <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Aneesh</a>' favorite movie? ✈️🚶‍♂️🏝️</p><p><br /></p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br /></p><p>Check out our new YouTube Channel:</p><p>https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub</p><p><br /></p><p>Interviewers: <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Yossi Garmazi</a> &amp; <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Jacob Chizunza, CA</a></p><p><br /></p><p><strong>#tech</strong> <strong>#ai</strong> <strong>#data</strong> <strong>#loyalty</strong> <strong>#IPO</strong></p><p><br /></p><p>Chapters:</p><p>00:00 Intro</p><p>01:03 entrepreneur by accident</p><p>02:05 envisioning a global firm</p><p>03:55: Pivots</p><p>6:45: When to pivot?</p><p>8:01: India next silicon valley</p><p>10:13: Step down and benefits for the transition</p><p>13:49: Why going public in India</p><p>16:16: AI challenges to the business</p><p>19:57: Capillary differentiator</p><p>24:05: Customer loyalty evolvements </p><p>26:17: Moving beyond customer loyalty</p><p>27:29: Examples of transformational customer use</p><p>30:02: Inner peace philosophy influence</p><p>33:00 Advice for entrepreneurs</p><p>34:25 Hobbies outside of work</p><p>34:55 Coffee with an historical figure</p><p>35:30 Alternative life to entrepreneurship</p>

✈️🚶‍♂️🏝️&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Check our new website:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;https://www.boardroomclub.net&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Check out our new YouTube Channel:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Interviewers: &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; rel=&quot;ugc noopener noreferrer&quot;&gt;Yossi Garmazi&lt;/a&gt; &amp;amp; &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; rel=&quot;ugc noopener noreferrer&quot;&gt;Jacob Chizunza, CA&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;#tech&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#ai&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#data&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#loyalty&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#IPO&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Chapters:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;00:00 Intro&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;01:03 entrepreneur by accident&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;02:05 envisioning a global firm&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;03:55: Pivots&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;6:45: When to pivot?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;8:01: India next silicon valley&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;10:13: Step down and benefits for the transition&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;13:49: Why going public in India&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;16:16: AI challenges to the business&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;19:57: Capillary differentiator&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;24:05: Customer loyalty evolvements &lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;26:17: Moving beyond customer loyalty&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;27:29: Examples of transformational customer use&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;30:02: Inner peace philosophy influence&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;33:00 Advice for entrepreneurs&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;34:25 Hobbies outside of work&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;34:55 Coffee with an historical figure&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;35:30 Alternative life to entrepreneurship&lt;/p&gt;

			<title><![CDATA[Voyantis CEO: We Are Prescriptive AI, Maximizing your GTM | Builders Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch a preview our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Ido Wiesenberg</a>, Co-Founder and CEO of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Voyantis</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p>As the <strong>#market</strong> has shifted from 'growth at all costs' to a focus on efficient growth, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Voyantis</a> entered the picture with a cutting-edge <strong>#platform</strong> designed to help brands acquire higher-value customers while maximizing <strong>#LTV</strong>.</p><p><br /></p><p>We started by discussing the <strong>#importance</strong> of finding the right <strong>#CoFounder</strong>, the idea of founder–market fit, and what the early days of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Voyantis</a> looked like at the beginning of 2020.</p><p><br /></p><p>This led us into a <strong>#CaseStudy</strong> on how the platform works in practice, using the food delivery space as an example. Powered by <strong>#AI</strong>, Voyantis’ platform operates across 2 main layers:</p><p><br /></p><p>1) <strong>#Prediction</strong> – Leveraging AI to analyze data and achieve up to 90% accuracy in predicting customer value.</p><p><br /></p><p>2) <strong>#Activation</strong> – Using a <strong>#SignalEngnerring</strong> to connect insights directly to marketing channels such as <a <strong>#smarter</strong> acquisition of future customers.</p><p><br /></p><p>We then explored their <strong>#GTM</strong> strategy and what differentiates <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Voyantis</a> from others in the space - particularly their positioning as a <strong>#PrescriptiveAI</strong> platform rather than just predictive AI.</p><p><br /></p><p>Beyond Voyantis, <a 			<title><![CDATA[EON CEO: You Could train AI Using Your Backups | Builders Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Ofir Ehrlich</a>, Co-Founder and CEO of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Eon.io</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p>As companies need more data to train their <strong>#agents</strong>, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Eon.io</a> has grown rapidly, <strong>#raising</strong> over $500M in just two years to chase a $100B+ market <strong>#opportunity</strong>.</p><p><br /></p><p>We explored how their platform enables organizations to retrieve and extract <strong>#data</strong> directly from cloud <strong>#backups</strong> with minimal overhead, while also indexing that data so it can be used for <strong>#AI</strong> and BI applications.</p><p><br /></p><p>One particularly compelling case study we touched on was their work with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">SoFi</a>, where <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Eon.io</a> reduced <strong>#data</strong> retrieval times from days to minutes and delivered over 100% <strong>#ROI</strong> in the first year.</p><p><br /></p><p>Interestingly, because the solution is <strong>#agentless</strong>, clients don’t need to install any <strong>#software</strong>. That led to a discussion about how the company <strong>#balances</strong> the access needed and data governance.</p><p><br /></p><p>We then explored how the <strong>#cloud</strong> is evolving into a “nervous system” for organizations, and whether this shift means organizations are moving away from buying <strong>#infrastructure</strong> like VMs and toward purchasing outcomes such as <strong>#automation</strong></p><p><br /></p><p>From there, we discussed the broader cloud <strong>#ecosystem</strong> and whether, in the near future, it will become easier for companies to move workloads between <strong>#cloud </strong>providers.</p><p><br /></p><p>Lastly, we touched on <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Ofir</a>’s angel <strong>#investments</strong>, including the green flags he looks for in founders, and the ultimate question: what matters more - choosing the right <strong>#team</strong> or the right <strong>#market</strong>?</p><p><br /></p><p>And take a guess what is <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Ofir</a> favorite <strong>#Movie</strong>? 🚀🌕🧑‍🚀</p><p><br /></p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br /></p><p>Check out our new YouTube Channel:</p><p>https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub</p><p><br /></p><p>Interviewers: <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Yossi Garmazi</a> &amp; <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Álvaro Jiménez Cabello de los Cobos</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p><strong>#tech</strong> <strong>#ai</strong> <strong>#data</strong> <strong>#cloud</strong> <strong>#backup </strong></p><p>Chapters:</p><p>00:00 Intro</p><p>01:04 Creating a Product in Data</p><p>03:11 Raising funds</p><p>06:34 Eon´s Products</p><p>09:48 Building Value in Data Recovery</p><p>13:27 Balancing safety and access</p><p>15:01 LLM Pipelines?</p><p>18:00 Evolution of data management in the age of AI</p><p>20:55 Cloud as a company´s nervous system</p><p>23:39 Cloud providers and standards</p><p>27:11 Green flags in Angel Investing</p><p>29:11 Exciting verticals to invest in</p><p>30:53 Advice for entrepreneurs</p><p>33:03 Hobbies outside of work</p><p>34:48 Coffee with a historical figure</p><p>35:37 Favorite Movie</p><p>35:49 Alternative life to entrepreneurship</p>

target=&quot;_blank&quot; rel=&quot;ugc noopener noreferrer&quot;&gt;Eon.io&lt;/a&gt; has grown rapidly, &lt;strong&gt;#raising&lt;/strong&gt; over $500M in just two years to chase a $100B+ market &lt;strong&gt;#opportunity&lt;/strong&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;We explored how their platform enables organizations to retrieve and extract &lt;strong&gt;#data&lt;/strong&gt; directly from cloud &lt;strong&gt;#backups&lt;/strong&gt; with minimal overhead, while also indexing that data so it can be used for &lt;strong&gt;#AI&lt;/strong&gt; and BI applications.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;One particularly compelling case study we touched on was their work with &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#&quot; rel=&quot;ugc noopener noreferrer&quot;&gt;Eon.io&lt;/a&gt; reduced &lt;strong&gt;#data&lt;/strong&gt; retrieval times from days to minutes and delivered over 100% &lt;strong&gt;#ROI&lt;/strong&gt; in the first year.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Interestingly, because the solution is &lt;strong&gt;#agentless&lt;/strong&gt;, clients don’t need to install any &lt;strong&gt;#software&lt;/strong&gt;. 			<title><![CDATA[AMD EVP: Data Center Solutions & AI | Builders Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Forrest Norrod</a>, EVP and GM of the Data Center Solutions Business Group at <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">AMD</a> (NASDAQ: AMD)</p><p><br /></p><p>As the world of <strong>#computing</strong> is changing at an unprecedented scale, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">AMD</a> sits right at the epicenter - driven by major recent <strong>#developments</strong> and strategic partnerships.</p><p><br /></p><p>We started by discussing <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Forrest</a>’s philosophy of being <strong>#customer</strong>-centric, and why this matters so much in this industry. Customers aren’t looking for a piece of <strong>#technology</strong> or silicon - they’re looking for complete <strong>#solutions</strong> to real problems.</p><p><br /></p><p>We then explored <strong>#datacenters</strong> in <strong>#space</strong>. Is it possible? Harnessing <strong>#solar</strong> energy is an obvious advantage, but there are serious <strong>#challenges</strong> - from the fragility of <strong>#GPUs</strong> in space environments to the complexity of dissipating heat from chips.</p><p><br /></p><p>As <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">AMD</a> evolved from a CPU challenger into an end-to-end data center platform company, we discussed what it took to make that <strong>#transition</strong> a reality - from adopting a <strong>#SystemsFirst</strong> engineering mindset to strategic moves like the $5B <strong>#acquisition</strong> of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">ZT Systems</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p>And with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">AMD</a>’s recent partnership with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">OpenAI</a>, where OpenAI is set to deploy <strong>#6gigawatts</strong> of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">AMD</a> GPUs, we dove into the <strong>#journey</strong> behind that collaboration and what it means for positioning AMD at the forefront of the <strong>#AI</strong> era.</p><p><br /></p><p>We also discussed how the role of the <strong>#builder</strong> is evolving given these technological shifts. And as a turn-based gamer, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Forrest</a> even shared that he writes <strong>#games</strong> himself - still waiting on that <strong>#demo</strong>, Forrest 😉</p><p><br /></p><p>Lastly, we touched on why all of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">AMD</a>’s server <strong>#CPUs</strong> are named after cities in <strong>#Italy</strong>, and wrapped up with <strong>#advice</strong> for current and future entrepreneurs navigating this new era of computing.</p><p><br /></p><p>And can you guess <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Forrest</a> favorite <strong>#movie</strong>? 🤖 🏙️🎬</p><p> </p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br /></p><p>Check out our new YouTube Channel:</p><p>https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub</p><p><br /></p><p>Interviewers: <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Yossi Garmazi</a> &amp; <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Álvaro Jiménez Cabello de los Cobos</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p><strong>#tech</strong> <strong>#ai</strong> <strong>#datacenter</strong> <strong>#agent</strong> <strong>#gamer </strong></p><p><br /></p><p><strong>Chapters:</strong></p><p>00:00 Intro</p><p>00:48 Building long term strategy</p><p>02:27 Non Obvious Lessons</p><p>04:44 Data Centers in Space</p><p>06:52 Internal Shifts in AMD</p><p>09:19 Impact of Shifts on Customer Relations</p><p>11:06 Impact of Memory Capacity</p><p>13:33 Selling to OpenAI</p><p>15:22 Effects of Customer Input</p><p>15:40 AGI</p><p>19:08 The Future of Coding</p><p>21:46 Projects outside of AMD</p><p>22:10 AI on Physical worlds</p><p>25:24 Favorite &amp; Most surprising Country</p><p>26:59 Hobbies and Passions</p><p>27:43 Advice for entrepreneurs</p><p>29:16 Career Mentor</p><p>30:30 Coffee with historical figure</p><p>31:39 Exciting Tech outside of AI</p><p>33:22 Favorite Movie</p>

			<title><![CDATA[Atlan Co-CEO: We Bring Context to AI | Builders Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Prukalpa</a> Sankar, Founder and Co-CEO of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Atlan</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p>What started as <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">SocialCops</a>, one of the largest data rollouts in <strong>#India’s</strong> history, evolved into <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Atlan</a> - a modern data infrastructure company.</p><p><br /></p><p>We started by exploring how the idea for <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Atlan</a> was born, including the early <strong>#product</strong> assumptions behind building a <strong>#data</strong> collaboration and metadata platform that enables companies to truly understand their data.</p><p><br /></p><p>We then discussed their early <strong>#sucess</strong>, growing organically from 100 to 500 users in just three months, and why they decided to <strong>#pivot</strong> and rebuild <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Atlan</a> - highlighting the critical importance of <strong>#integrations</strong> and change management.</p><p><br /></p><p>With the growing gap between <strong>#AI’s</strong> potential and the actual <strong>#ROI</strong> from it, we talked about the <strong>#AIchasm</strong> - from challenges around data readiness, context, and governance, to how solving these issues drives higher adoption.</p><p><br /></p><p>As <strong>#context</strong> is king in <strong>#AI</strong>, we dove into one of their most revolutionary products, <strong>#AtlanAI</strong>, which brings business context directly into AI use cases.</p><p><br /></p><p>Lastly, we from failing an <strong>#exam</strong>, to figuring out which <strong>#party</strong> to go to tonight, to selling your <strong>#bike</strong> on campus.</p><p><br></p><p>We then explored their <strong>#expansion</strong> strategy, including products like <strong>#GlobalFizz</strong>, which extends the social experience even after graduation.</p><p><br></p><p>With <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Fizz</a> ads generating a 3%-18% <strong>#CTR</strong>, we dove into what drives that success - from working closely with <strong>#brands</strong> students love like <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Perplexity</a>, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Quizlet</a> and <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Sony</a>, to carefully crafting <strong>#ads</strong> that add value without inundating the feed.</p><p><br></p><p>Another interesting point was their operational <strong>#discipline</strong>: keeping the majority of their $25M <strong>#SeriesB</strong> still in the <strong>#bank</strong>, running a lean team of ~30 employees, and spending very little on <strong>#marketing</strong>.</p><p><br></p><p>Lastly, we discussed their decision to <strong>#relocate</strong> from SF to <strong>#NYC</strong>, where it’s easier to build a startup today? how user <strong>#behavior</strong> has changed over the last five years? and their latest product addition - <strong>#FizzMarketplace</strong>.</p><p><br></p><p>And guess <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Teddy</a>’s alternate-universe <strong>#profession</strong>? 🏪👨‍🍳🍳</p><p><br></p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br></p><p>Check out our new YouTube Channel:</p><p>https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub</p><p><br></p><p>Interviewers: <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Yossi Garmazi</a> &amp; <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Amado Vargas IV</a>.</p><p><br></p><p><strong>#tech #social #startup #entrepreneur #pancake</strong></p><p>Chapters:</p><p>00:00 Intro</p><p>00:44 Fizz&#39;s Beginnings</p><p>03:10 Sports Journalism Background</p><p>05:30 How Fizz helps Consumers</p><p>07:07 Global Fizz </p><p>09:18 Selecting Ad Partners (Monetization)</p><p>11:29 Maintaining and Balancing Growth</p><p>13:34 Early Monetization Mistakes?</p><p>15:52 Transition from SF to NYC</p><p>20:04 Any difficulties in Changing Location?</p><p>21:31 &quot;Bringing Social back to Social Media&quot;</p><p>24:45 Any Surprises in User Behavior?</p><p>26:24 Building Independently/Organic Growth</p><p>28:48 Impactful Mentor?</p><p>30:45 Personal Hobbies</p><p>32:23 Alternate Career Path?</p><p>33:40 Favorite Movie!</p>

			<title><![CDATA[Zapier CEO: Agents, PLG and Fully remote | Builders Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Wade Foster</a>, Co-Founder and CEO of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Zapier</a>.</p><p><br></p><p>Raising only about $1M, staying fully <strong>#remote</strong>, and reaching a <strong>#valuation</strong> of more than $5B - this is the story of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Zapier</a>.</p><p><br></p><p>We started by exploring <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Zapier</a>’s decision to be fully <strong>#remote</strong> from day one: from the philosophy of “hire when it hurts,” to documenting all communication across tools, and now taking it a step further by using <strong>#agents</strong> to activate that knowledge and drive a new level of <strong>#effectiveness</strong>.</p><p><br></p><p>We then dove into how <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Zapier</a> adapted to the <strong>#AI</strong> era - from the co-founders going back to the ground floor, to declaring “<strong>#CodeRed”</strong> after <strong>#customer</strong> conversations, recognizing the <strong>#disruption</strong> potential, and ultimately launching <strong>#ZapierAgents</strong> and <strong>#MCP</strong>.</p><p><br></p><p>Next, we discussed their decision to grow not only through <strong>#PLG</strong>, but also with a dedicated <strong>#sales</strong> motion - what led them there, and the <strong>#mindset</strong> shifts required to make it work.</p><p><br></p><p>As the world evolves, we asked: who is the <strong>#automation</strong> hero of <strong>#2026</strong>? The builder, or the manager of agents? 			<title><![CDATA[LinkedIn CPO: 70% of Job Skills Will Change by 2030! | Builders Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Tomer Cohen</a>, Chief Product Officer of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">LinkedIn</a>.</p><p><br></p><p>With over 1B <strong>#users</strong> on the platform - and as the <strong>#JobMarket</strong> is being reshaped by <strong>#AI</strong>- there is no better person to host than the one building <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">LinkedIn</a>.</p><p><br></p><p>We started by discussing the evolving job market, new <strong>#AI</strong>-driven job verticals, and one of the <strong>#fastest</strong>-growing areas: <strong>#training</strong> AI models across <strong>#industries</strong> - from legal and sports to many others.</p><p><br></p><p>We then turned to one of Tomer’s favorite <strong>#quotes</strong>:</p><p>“We might be wrong, but not confused.”</p><p><br></p><p>This led us into a thoughtful discussion on <strong>#AI</strong>: 🤖⚖️</p><p> Is it morally <strong>#good</strong> or <strong>#bad</strong>? A moral actor in itself - or simply a <strong>#technology</strong> whose impact depends on how we choose to <strong>#deploy</strong> it?</p><p><br></p><p>We then explored some fascinating <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">LinkedIn</a> data:</p><p>📊 By <strong>#2030</strong>, 70% of the <strong>#skills</strong> required to do <strong>#today’s</strong> jobs will change</p><p>🚀 70% of the most in-demand <strong>#roles</strong> in LinkedIn’s &#39;Jobs on the Rise report&#39; didn’t <strong>#exist</strong> the year before</p><p> 🔁 Someone entering the <strong>#JobMarket</strong> today will hold 2× as many jobs as someone did 15 years ago</p><p><br></p><p>From there, we explored <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">LinkedIn</a>’s <strong>#FullStackBuilderProgram</strong> 🧩: </p><p>Building end-to-end products, finding product-market fit, and why <strong>#meritocracy</strong> matters more than ever.</p><p><br></p><p>We then zoomed out to discuss <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">LinkedIn</a>’s product <strong>#evolution</strong> 🔄: from a job marketplace to a <strong>#platform</strong> for sharing <strong>#ideas</strong> - and how <strong>#video</strong> has become the <strong>#fastest</strong>-growing format, growing 40% YoY 🎥📈</p><p><br></p><p>And Can you guess <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/?share=true#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Tomer</a>’s favorite <strong>#movie</strong>? 			<title><![CDATA[BrightAI CEO: We Bootstrapped to $80M! | Builders Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Alex Hawkinson</a>, Founder and CEO of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">BrightAI</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p>How many companies do you know that <strong>#bootstrapped</strong> their way to $80M in <strong>#ARR</strong> while truly changing the world? This is the story of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">BrightAI</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p>From realizing we haven’t truly graduated from <strong>#Roman</strong>-era <strong>#infrastructure</strong> maintenance, <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">BrightAI</a> set out to change it - using cutting-edge <strong>#observability</strong> tools to see in real time, for example, when storms hit, where leaks occur, and which power poles are down.</p><p><br /></p><p>That naturally led us to discuss their <strong>#StatefulOS</strong> Physical AI platform, which uses <strong>#Edge</strong> AI to deliver continuous, 24/7, real-time diagnostics - via <strong>#robots</strong> or even <strong>#wearable</strong> tools. The result? Precise, surgical action items and a doubling of <strong>#productivity</strong>.</p><p><br /></p><p>We also touched on <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">BrightAI</a>’s <strong>#bootstrapped</strong> journey to $80M ARR - and whether such <strong>#growth</strong> is still possible today, or if <strong>#VibeCoding</strong> tools are only suitable for <strong>#MVPs</strong>.</p><p><br /></p><p>From there, we explored a pivotal early decision: going <strong>#MultiVertical</strong> versus <strong>#horizontal</strong>. What were the tradeoffs, and why was this choice <strong>#essential</strong> to their success?</p><p><br /></p><p>And with discussions about <strong>#DataCenters</strong> in <strong>#space</strong>, what role could <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">BrightAI</a> play? Turns out <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Alex</a> also founded another company- <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Observable Space</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p>Lastly, guess what is <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Alex</a>' favorite movie? ⚔️💥🎬</p><p><br /></p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br /></p><p>Check out our new YouTube Channel:</p><p>https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub</p><p><br /></p><p>Interviewers: <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Yossi Garmazi</a> &amp; <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Jason Woolf</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p><strong>#tech</strong> <strong>#observability</strong> <strong>#ai</strong> <strong>#infrastructure</strong> <strong>#Pedro</strong></p><p><strong>Chapters:</strong></p><p>00:00 Intro</p><p>00:56 Robots in the Streets?</p><p>01:57 From Smart Things to BrightAI</p><p>03:26 BrightAI' product assumptions</p><p>05:05 The rise of LLMs</p><p>06:23 Bright AI: Observability</p><p>08:39 Stateful OS Platform</p><p>12:04 Bright AI &amp; Space</p><p>13:36 Efficient Computer Partnership</p><p>16:06 Bootstrapping to $80M ARR</p><p>19:22 Multi Vertical Strategy</p><p>23:04 Future of Smart City</p><p>26:04 Observable Space</p><p>27:11 Wearable AI</p><p>29:35 Entrepreneurs Advice</p><p>32:14 Career Mentor</p><p>33:54 Coffee with Historical Figure</p><p>34:44 Napoleon Dynamite Movie</p>

⚔️💥🎬&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Check our new website:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;https://www.boardroomclub.net&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Check out our new YouTube Channel:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Interviewers: &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; rel=&quot;ugc noopener noreferrer&quot;&gt;Yossi Garmazi&lt;/a&gt; &amp;amp; &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; rel=&quot;ugc noopener noreferrer&quot;&gt;Jason Woolf&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br /&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;#tech&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#observability&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#ai&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#infrastructure&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#Pedro&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Chapters:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;00:00 Intro&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;00:56 Robots in the Streets?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;01:57 From Smart Things to BrightAI&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;03:26 BrightAI&apos; product assumptions&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;05:05 The rise of LLMs&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;06:23 Bright AI: Observability&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;08:39 Stateful OS Platform&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;12:04 Bright AI &amp;amp; Space&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;13:36 Efficient Computer Partnership&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;16:06 Bootstrapping to $80M ARR&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;19:22 Multi Vertical Strategy&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;23:04 Future of Smart City&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;26:04 Observable Space&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;27:11 Wearable AI&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;29:35 Entrepreneurs Advice&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;32:14 Career Mentor&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;33:54 Coffee with Historical Figure&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;34:44 Napoleon Dynamite Movie&lt;/p&gt;

			<title><![CDATA[Houzz Executive Chair Adi Tatarko: We Bootstrapped to 350K Users, Now we are 70M | Builders Series]]></title>
			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch a preview of our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Adi Tatarko</a>, Co-Founder and Executive Chair of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Houzz</a>.</p><p><br></p><p><a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Houzz</a> has 70 <strong>#million</strong> home design enthusiasts and 3 million professionals - but did you know it was <strong>#bootstrapped</strong> for 18 months?</p><p><br></p><p>We started by discussing the decision to bootstrap all the way to 350K <strong>#users</strong>, and why that phase was <strong>#pivotal</strong> to <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Houzz</a>’s early <strong>#success</strong> before bringing in <strong>#VCs</strong> like <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">oren zeev</a> and <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Sequoia Capital</a>.</p><p><br></p><p>That naturally led to a conversation about their choice to remain <strong>#private</strong>, and how that has been <strong>#instrumental</strong> to long-term, <strong>#strategic</strong> thinking.</p><p><br></p><p>We then explored <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Houzz</a>’s <strong>#community</strong>-led growth model—how they <strong>#pioneered</strong> it, how they implemented it, and the impact it had on <strong>#product</strong> development, from structured timelines for designers to the integration of powerful <strong>#AI</strong> tools.</p><p><br></p><p>Finally, we touched on how <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Houzz</a> evolves its products, like <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Houzz Pro</a>, making <strong>#design</strong> more <strong>#fun</strong> and productive through tools like <strong>#CRM</strong>, and adjusting features as the company <strong>#expands</strong> globally.</p><p><br></p><p>And <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/preload/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Adi</a>&#39;s favorite movie? 🌊🏝️🏃‍♂️</p><p><br></p><p>Check our new noreferrer">Amado Vargas IV</a>.</p><p><br></p><p><strong>#tech</strong> <strong>#innovation</strong> <strong>#ai</strong> <strong>#design</strong> 			<description><![CDATA[<p>Watch our exclusive interview with <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Andrew Feldman</a>, Founder and CEO of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Cerebras</a>.</p><p><br></p><p>The <strong>#chip</strong> company <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Cerebras</a> just raised $1.1B at an $8.1B valuation.</p><p><br></p><p>We started by exploring a bold idea: <strong>#DataCenters</strong> in <strong>#space</strong>. Is it feasible? Probably not anytime soon. But every <strong>#contrarian</strong> idea needs a beginning, and such the story of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Cerebras</a>. We discussed why the team chose not to redesign the <strong>#GPU</strong>, but to build an entirely <strong>#new</strong> chip.</p><p><br></p><p>From the early days of burning $8M a month for 18 months, to one <strong>#pivotal</strong> Tuesday, when everything finally started to work, on a sunny day in <strong>#LosAltos</strong>, California</p><p><br></p><p>The result: Wafer-Scale Engine (<strong>#WSE</strong>) - <strong>#LargestChip</strong> in the World - 46,225mm2. </p><p><br></p><p>With newly $1.1B <strong>#funding</strong> round we explored the areas <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Cerebras</a> plans to push forward next: faster <strong>#computation</strong>, more efficient <strong>#storage</strong>, and higher-speed data movement of the chip.</p><p><br></p><p>Lastly, we discussed what <strong>#product</strong> assumptions Andrew had to unlearn to take <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Cerebras</a> to the next level - and the <strong>#advice</strong> he would give to <strong>#entrepreneurs</strong> today.</p><p><br></p><p>And <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Andrew</a>&#39;s favorite movie and TV series? 🤖🕵️‍♂️🎬</p><p><br></p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br></p><p>Check out our new YouTube Channel:</p><p>https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub</p><p><br></p><p>Interviewers: <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Yossi Garmazi</a> &amp; <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Amado Vargas IV</a>.</p><p><br></p><p><strong>#tech</strong> <strong>#innovation</strong> <strong>#ai</strong> <strong>#chip</strong> <strong>#hardware</strong></p><p><br></p><p><strong>Chapters:</strong></p><p>00:00 Intro</p><p>00:50 AI Bottlenecks?</p><p>03:35 GPU Foundation</p><p>11:07 Future Areas of Development?</p><p>12:31 Adapting to the Market</p><p>14:25 G-42 Partnership</p><p>16:17 Data Center Expansion </p><p>18:26 Geopolitical Interests</p><p>20:33 Sovereign Demand and International Expansion</p><p>22:38 Data Centers in Space?</p><p>26:12 Hardware Foundation and Handling Market Hype</p><p>29:19 Establishing Fundamentals</p><p>31:39 Advice for Entrepreneurs</p><p>33:13 Influential Mentor?</p><p>35:48 Coffee with a Historical Figure?</p><p>38:23 Favorite Movie &amp; TV Series</p>

🤖🕵️‍♂️🎬&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Check our new website:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;https://www.boardroomclub.net&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Check out our new YouTube Channel:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Interviewers: &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; rel=&quot;ugc noopener noreferrer&quot;&gt;Yossi Garmazi&lt;/a&gt; &amp;amp; &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; rel=&quot;ugc noopener noreferrer&quot;&gt;Amado Vargas IV&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;#tech&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#innovation&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#ai&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#chip&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;#hardware&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Chapters:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;00:00 Intro&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;00:50 AI Bottlenecks?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;03:35 GPU Foundation&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;11:07 Future Areas of Development?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;12:31 Adapting to the Market&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;14:25 G-42 Partnership&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;16:17 Data Center Expansion &lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;18:26 Geopolitical Interests&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;20:33 Sovereign Demand and International Expansion&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;22:38 Data Centers in Space?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;26:12 Hardware Foundation and Handling Market Hype&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;29:19 Establishing Fundamentals&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;31:39 Advice for Entrepreneurs&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;33:13 Influential Mentor?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;35:48 Coffee with a Historical Figure?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;38:23 Favorite Movie &amp;amp; TV Series&lt;/p&gt;

Gurugram commute to India’s <strong>#leading</strong> audio fiction <strong>#platform</strong>: The Story of <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Pocket FM</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p>We started by exploring <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Pocket FM</a>’s early days - From taking a more <strong>#strategic</strong> approach to understanding that sales is a business <strong>#consulting</strong> role, to helping customers succeed by harnessing <strong>#technology</strong> - and yes, he even wrote a <strong>#book</strong> about it!</p><p><br /></p><p>We then discussed how the art of <strong>#sales</strong> differs across <strong>#geographies</strong> like the US, EMEA, and APAC, which naturally led to a broader conversation about <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Sierra</a>’s unique <strong>#sales</strong> structure, organized by <strong>#verticals</strong>.</p><p><br /></p><p>Since Sierra operates in <strong>#ConversationalAI</strong>, we debated the <strong>#future</strong> of sales: will <strong>#humans</strong> still be needed? And what is Sierra’s “Seller of the Future” concept? They even have a sales agent named <strong>#ReggieJunior</strong>!</p><p><br /></p><p>Lastly, we touched on the <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">SiriusXM</a> case study and how their <strong>#HarmonyAgent</strong> - powered by <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Sierra</a> - can handle <strong>#chatting</strong>, <strong>#negotiation</strong>, or even calling a satellite company for you.</p><p><br /></p><p>And <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Reggie</a>'s favorite movie? 🎬💼🌟</p><p><br /></p><p>Check our new website:</p><p>https://www.boardroomclub.net</p><p><br /></p><p>Check out our new YouTube Channel:</p><p>https://www.youtube.com/@BoardroomClub</p><p><br /></p><p>Interviewers: <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Yossi Garmazi</a> &amp; <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/102144486/admin/page-posts/published/#" target="_blank" rel="ugc noopener noreferrer">Álvaro Jiménez Cabello de los Cobos</a>.</p><p><br /></p><p><strong>#tech</strong> <strong>#innovation</strong> <strong>#ai</strong> <strong>#sales</strong> <strong>#strategy </strong></p><p><br /></p><p><strong>Chapters:</strong></p><p><strong>Chapters:</strong></p><p>00:00 Intro</p><p>01:30 Sales in Academia</p><p>03:05 Why Sierra?</p><p>05:20 Sales Philosophy</p><p>06:49 Who are the best salespeople?</p><p>09:00 Writing Sales Book</p><p>12:47Keeping sales teams focused</p><p>14:54 Adjusting sales method</p><p>17:09 Important qualities in team members</p><p>18:50 Human vs AI Seller Agents</p><p>21:40 Capabilities for AI Agents</p><p>23:48 Sierra clients: SiriusXM &amp; Rocket Mortgage</p><p>25:42 Monetizing AI Agent Sales</p><p>26:52 Advice for Jr Salespeople</p><p>28:24 Impactful Mentors</p><p>29:55 Staying motivated</p><p>32:12 Hobbies</p><p>32:57 Alternative Career</p><p>33:43 Favorite Movies</p><p>35:21 Classmate in Shark Tank!</p>
